\\\"crm_perdof\\\", \\\"acr_vla\\\", \\\"crm_obs_only\\\", \\\"reconvla\\\", \\\"random_selection\\\", \\\"adaptive_k\\\", \\\"adaptive_unc_head\\\", \\\"adaptive_conformal_crm\\\"])\\n mean (K=10 avg)\\\")\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--smolvla_path\\\", type=str, default=\\\"/root/autodl-tmp/models/smolvla_libero/\\\")\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--model\\\", type=str, default=\\\"smolvla\\\",\\n                   choices=[\\\"smolvla\\\", \\\"pi05\\\"],\\n                   help=\\\"VLA model backbone\\\")\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--pi05_path\\\", type=str,\\n                   default=\\\"/root/autodl-tmp/models/pi05_libero_finetuned/\\\",\\n                   help=\\\"Path to PI05 model (used when --model pi05)\\\")\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--obs_dim\\\", type=int, default=OBS_DIM)\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--hidden_dim\\\", type=int, default=256)\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--task_filter\\\", t\\n\\n... [截断: 输出过长，省略 430 字符] ...\\n\\nckpoint value)')\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--adaptive_k\\\", action=\\\"store_true\\\",\\n                   help=\\\"Enable adaptive K sampling (start with k_init, expand to k_full if needed)\\\")\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--adaptive_threshold\\\", type=float, default=0.1,\\n                   help=\\\"L2 distance threshold for adaptive K branching\\\")\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--k_init\\\", type=int, default=2,\\n                   help=\\\"Initial sample count for adaptive K\\\")\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--k_full\\\", type=int, default=5,\\n                   help=\\\"Full sample count when adaptive K expands\\\")\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--unc_head_checkpoint\\\", type=str, default=None,\\n                   help=\\\"Path to trained UncertaintyHead model checkpoint\\\")\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--unc_threshold\\\", type=float, default=0.5,\\n                   help=\\\"Uncertainty threshold tau: u > tau triggers CRM correction\\\")\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--conformal_threshold\\\", type=float, default=0.074855,\\n                   help=\\\"Conformal threshold tau for K=2 L2 distance (P50)\\\")\\n    return p.parse_args()\\n\\n\\ndef get_robot_state(obs):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Extract 8D robot state: eef_pos(3) + eef_axisangle(3) + gripper_qpos(2).\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    eef_pos = obs[\\\"robot0_eef_pos\\\"]\\n    eef_axisangle = quat2axisangle(obs[\\\"robot0_eef_quat\\\"])\\n    gripper_qpos = obs[\\\"robot0_gripper_qpos\\\"]\\n    return np.concatenate([eef_pos, eef_axisangle, gripper_qpos]).astype(np.float64)\\n\\n\\ndef load_libero_env(benchmark_name):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Load LIBERO benchmark tasks and create environments.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    from libero.libero import benchmark as bm\\n    from libero.libero.envs import OffScreenRenderEnv\\n\\n    benchmark_dict = {\\n        \\\"spatial\\\": \\\"libero_spatial\\\",\\n        \\\"object\\\": \\\"libero_object\\\",\\n        \\\"goal\\\": \\\"libero_goal\\\",\\n        \\\"long\\\": \\\"libero_10\\\",\\n        \\\"90\\\": \\\"libero_90\\\",\\n    }\\n    bm_name = benchmark_dict[benchmark_name]\\n    benchmark_instance = bm.get_benchmark(bm_name)()\\n    n_tasks = benchmark_instance.n_tasks\\n\\n    task_envs = []\\n    for task_idx in range(n_tasks):\\n        task = benchmark_instance.get_task(task_idx)\\n        task_name = task.name\\n        task_description = task.language\\n        task_bddl = benchmark_instance.get_task_bddl_file_path(task_idx)\\n\\n        env_args = {\\n            \\\"bddl_file_name\\\": task_bddl,\\n            \\\"camera_heights\\\": 256,\\n            \\\"camera_widths\\\": 256,\\n            \\\"camera_names\\\": [\\\"agentview\\\", \\\"robot0_eye_in_hand\\\"],\\n            \\\"render_gpu_device_id\\\": int(os.environ.get(\\\"MUJOCO_EGL_DEVICE_ID\\\", \\\"0\\\")),\\n        }\\n        env = OffScreenRenderEnv(**env_args)\\n        env.seed(42)\\n\\n        task_envs.append({\\n            \\\"task_name\\\": task_name,\\n            \\\"task_description\\\": task_description,\\n            \\\"env\\\": env,\\n            \\\"init_states\\\": benchmark_instance.get_task_init_states(task_idx),\\n        })\\n\\n    return task_envs\\n\\n\\ndef select_action_vla_only(wrapper, obs, task_name, K, cgs):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Baseline: mean of K samples (full 7D), no CRM.\\n\\n    For chunk-emitting policies (PI05) that expose predict_action(), we replay\\n    the cached chunk open-loop instead of re-running inference every env step\\n    and only consuming action[0]. The latter starves multi-step behaviors like\\n    the gripper close at chunk step ~20 -- the root cause of 0% SR on PI05.\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    agentview = obs[\\\"agentview_image\\\"]\\n    eye_in_hand = obs.get(\\\"robot0_eye_in_hand_image\\\", None)\\n    robot_state = get_robot_state(obs)\\n\\n    if hasattr(wrapper, \\\"predict_action\\\"):\\n        return wrapper.predict_action(\\n            agentview_rgb=agentview,\\n            task_name=task_name,\\n            robot_state=robot_state,\\n            eye_in_hand_rgb=eye_in_hand,\\n            K=K,\\n        )\\n\\n    obs_feat, samples = wrapper.get_obs_features_and_samples(\\n        agentview_rgb=agentview,\\n        task_name=task_name,\\n        robot_state=robot_state,\\n        eye_in_hand_rgb=eye_in_hand,\\n        K=K,\\n    )\\n    # samples: (1, K, chunk_size, FULL_ACTION_DIM)\\n    k_trunc = samples[0, :, :H_EFF, :]  # (K, H_eff, 7)\\n    mean_action = k_trunc.mean(dim=0)  # (H_eff, 7)\\n    return mean_action[0].cpu().numpy()\\n\\n\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.34}}"}}}

permissions and\\\\n# limitations under the License.\\\\n\\\\nfrom copy import deepcopy\\\\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass\\\\nfrom typing import Any\\\\n\\\\nimport numpy as np\\\\nimport torch\\\\n\\\\nfrom lerobot.configs import PipelineFeatureType, PolicyFeature\\\\nfrom lerobot.processor import (\\\\n    AbsoluteActionsProcessorStep,\\\\n    AddBatchDimensionProcessorStep,\\\\n    DeviceProcessorStep,\\\\n    NormalizerProcessorStep,\\\\n    PolicyAction,\\\\n    PolicyProcessorPipeline,\\\\n    ProcessorStep,\\\\n    ProcessorStepRegistry,\\\\n    RelativeActionsProcessorStep,\\\\n    RenameObservationsProcessorStep,\\\\n    TokenizerProcessorStep,\\\\n    UnnormalizerProcessorStep,\\\\n    policy_action_to_transition,\\\\n    transition_to_policy_action,\\\\n)\\\\nfrom lerobot.types import EnvTransition, TransitionKey\\\\nfrom lerobot.utils.constants import (\\\\n    OBS_STATE,\\\\n    POLICY_POSTPROCESSOR_DEFAULT_NAME,\\\\n    POLICY_PREPROCESSOR_DEFAULT_NAME,\\\\n)\\\\n\\\\nfrom .configuration_pi05 import PI05Config\\\\n\\\\n\\\\n@ProcessorStepRegistry.register(name=\\\\\\\"pi05_prepare_state_tokenizer_processor_step\\\\\\\")\\\\n@dataclass\\\\nclass Pi05PrepareStateTokenizerProcessorStep(ProcessorStep):\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    Processor step to prepare the state and tokenize the language input.\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n    max_state_dim: int = 32\\\\n    task_key: str = \\\\\\\"task\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n    def __call__(self, transition: EnvTransition) -> EnvTransition:\\\\n        transition = transition.copy()\\\\n\\\\n        state = transition.get(TransitionKey.OBSERVATION, {}).get(OBS_STATE)\\\\n        if state is None:\\\\n            raise ValueError(\\\\\\\"State is required for PI05\\\\\\\")\\\\n        tasks = transition.get(TransitionKey.COMPLEMENTARY_DATA, {}).get(self.task_key)\\\\n        if tasks is None:\\\\n            raise ValueError(\\\\\\\"No task found in complementary data\\\\\\\")\\\\n\\\\n        # TODO: check if this necessary\\\\n        state = deepcopy(state)\\\\n\\\\n        # State should already be normalized to [-1, 1] by the NormalizerProcessorStep that runs before this step\\\\n        # Discretize into 256 bins (see openpi `PaligemmaTokenizer.tokenize()`)\\\\n        state_np = state.cpu().numpy()\\\\n        discretized_states = np.digitize(state_np, bins=np.linspace(-1, 1, 256 + 1)[:-1]) - 1\\\\n\\\\n        full_prompts = []\\\\n        for i, task in enumerate(tasks):\\\\n            cleaned_text = task.strip().replace(\\\\\\\"_\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\" \\\\\\\").replace(\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\" \\\\\\\")\\\\n            state_str = \\\\\\\" \\\\\\\".join(map(str, discretized_states[i]))\\\\n            full_prompt = f\\\\\\\"Task: {cleaned_text}, State: {state_str};\\\\\\\\nAction: \\\\\\\"\\\\n            full_prompts.append(full_prompt)\\\\n\\\\n        transition[TransitionKey.COMPLEMENTARY_DATA][self.task_key] = full_prompts\\\\n        # Normalize state to [-1, 1] range if needed (assuming it's already normalized by normalizer processor step!!)\\\\n        # Discretize into 256 bins (see openpi `PaligemmaTokenizer.tokenize()`)\\\\n        return transition\\\\n\\\\n    def transform_features(\\\\n        self, features: dict[PipelineFeatureType, dict[str, PolicyFeature]]\\\\n    ) -> dict[PipelineFeatureType, dict[str, PolicyFeature]]:\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        This step does not alter the feature definitions.\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        return features\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef make_pi05_pre_post_processors(\\\\n    config: PI05Config,\\\\n    dataset_stats: dict[str, dict[str, torch.Tensor]] | None = None,\\\\n) -> tuple[\\\\n    PolicyProcessorPipeline[dict[str, Any], dict[str, Any]],\\\\n    PolicyProcessorPipeline[PolicyAction, PolicyAction],\\\\n]:\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    Constructs pre-processor and post-processor pipelines for the PI0 policy.\\\\n\\\\n    The pre-processing pipeline prepares input data for the model by:\\\\n    1. Renaming features to match pretrained configurations.\\\\n    2. Normalizing input and output features based on dataset statistics.\\\\n    3. Adding a batch dimension.\\\\n    4. Appending a newline character to the task description for tokenizer compatibility.\\\\n    5. Tokenizing the text prompt using the PaliGemma tokenizer.\\\\n    6. Moving all data to the specified device.\\\\n\\\\n    The post-processing pipeline handles the model's output by:\\\\n    1. Moving data to the CPU.\\\\n    2. Unnormalizing the output features to their original scale.\\\\n\\\\n    Args:\\\\n        config: The configuration object for the PI0 policy.\\\\n        dataset_stats: A dictionary of statistics for normalization.\\\\n        preprocessor_kwargs: Additional arguments for the pre-processor pipeline.\\\\n        postprocessor_kwargs: Additional arguments for the post-processor pipeline.\\\\n\\\\n    Returns:\\\\n        A tuple containing the configured pre-processor and post-processor pipelines.\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n    relative_step = RelativeActionsProcessorStep(\\\\n        enabled=config.use_relative_actions,\\\\n        exclude_joints=getattr(config, \\\\\\\"relative_exclude_joints\\\\\\\", []),\\\\n        action_names=getattr(config, \\\\\\\"action_feature_names\\\\\\\", None),\\\\n    )\\\\n\\\\n    # OpenPI order: raw → relative → normalize → model → unnormalize → absolute\\\\n    input_steps: list[ProcessorStep] = [\\\\n        RenameObservationsProcessorStep(rename_map={}),  # To mimic the same processor as pretrained one\\\\n        AddBatchDimensionProcessorStep(),\\\\n        relative_step,\\\\n        # NOTE: NormalizerProcessorStep MUST come before Pi05PrepareStateTokenizerProcessorStep\\\\n        # because the tokenizer step expects normalized state in [-1, 1] range for discretization\\\\n        NormalizerProcessorStep(\\\\n            features={**config.input_features, **config.output_features},\\\\n            norm_map=config.normalization_mapping,\\\\n            stats=dataset_stats,\\\\n        ),\\\\n        Pi05PrepareStateTokenizerProcessorStep(max_state_dim=config.max_state_dim),\\\\n        TokenizerProcessorStep(\\\\n            tokenizer_name=\\\\\\\"google/paligemma-3b-pt-224\\\\\\\",\\\\n            max_length=config.tokenizer_max_length,\\\\n            padding_side=\\\\\\\"right\\\\\\\",\\\\n            padding=\\\\\\\"max_length\\\\\\\",\\\\n        ),\\\\n        DeviceProcessorStep(device=config.device),\\\\n    ]\\\\n\\\\n    output_steps: list[ProcessorStep] = [\\\\n        UnnormalizerProcessorStep(\\\\n            features=config.output_features, norm_map=config.normalization_mapping, stats=dataset_stats\\\\n        ),\\\\n        AbsoluteActionsProcessorStep(enabled=config.use_relative_actions, relative_step=relative_step),\\\\n        DeviceProcessorStep(device=\\\\\\\"cpu\\\\\\\"),\\\\n    ]\\\\n\\\\n    return (\\\\n        PolicyProcessorPipeline[dict[str, Any], dict[str, Any]](\\\\n            steps=input_steps,\\\\n            name=POLICY_PREPROCESSOR_DEFAULT_NAME,\\\\n        ),\\\\n        PolicyProcessorPipeline[PolicyAction, PolicyAction](\\\\n            steps=output_steps,\\\\n            name=POLICY_POSTPROCESSOR_DEFAULT_NAME,\\\\n            to_transition=policy_action_to_transition,\\\\n            to_output=transition_to_policy_action,\\\\n        ),\\\\n    )\\\\n\\\", \\\"stderr\\\": \\\"\\\", \\\"return_code\\\": 0}, \\\"error\\\": \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    Processor step to prepare the state and tokenize the language input.\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    max_state_dim: int = 32\\n    task_key: str = \\\"task\\\"\\n\\n    def f\\\"Task: {cleaned_text}, State: {state_str};\\\\nAction: \\\"\\n            full_prompts.append(full_prompt)\\n\\n        transition[TransitionKey.COMPLEMENTARY_DATA][self.task_key] = full_prompts\\n        # Normalize state to [-1, 1] range if needed (assuming it's already normalized by normalizer processor step!!)\\n        # Discretize into 256 bins (see openpi `PaligemmaTokenizer.tokenize()`)\\n        return transition\\n\\n    def transform_features(\\n        self, features: dict[PipelineFeatureType, dict[str, PolicyFeature]]\\n    ) -> dict[PipelineFeatureType, dict[str, PolicyFeature]]:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        This step does not alter the feature definitions.\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        return features\\n\\n\\ndef make_pi05_pre_post_processors(\\n    config: PI05Config,\\n    dataset_stats: dict[str, dict[str, torch.Tensor]] | None = None,\\n) -> tuple[\\n    PolicyProcessorPipeline[dict[str, Any], dict[str, Any]],\\n    PolicyProcessorPipeline[PolicyAction, PolicyAction],\\n]:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    Constructs pre-processor and post-processor pipelines for the PI0 policy.\\n\\n    The pre-processing pipeline prepares input data for the model by:\\n    1. Renaming features to match pretrained configurations.\\n    2. Normalizing input and output features based on dataset statistics.\\n    3. Adding a batch dimension.\\n    4. Appending a newline character to the task description for tokenizer compatibility.\\n    5. Tokenizing the text prompt using the PaliGemma tokenizer.\\n    6. Moving all data to the specified device.\\n\\n    The post-processing pipeline handles the model's output by:\\n    1. Moving data to the CPU.\\n    2. Unnormalizing the output features to their original scale.\\n\\n    Args:\\n        config: The configuration object for the PI0 policy.\\n        dataset_stats: A dictionary of statistics for normalization.\\n        preprocessor_kwargs: Additional arguments for the pre-processor pipeline.\\n        postprocessor_kwargs: Additional arguments for the post-processor pipeline.\\n\\n    Returns:\\n        A tuple containing the configured pre-processor and post-processor pipelines.\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    relative_step = RelativeActionsProcessorStep(\\n        enabled=config.use_relative_actions,\\n        exclude_joints=getattr(config, \\\"relative_exclude_joints\\\", []),\\n        action_names=getattr(config, \\\"action_feature_names\\\", None),\\n    )\\n\\n    # OpenPI order: raw → relative → normalize → model → unnormalize → absolute\\n    input_steps: list[ProcessorStep] = [\\n        RenameObservationsProcessorStep(rename_map={}),  # To mimic the same processor as pretrained one\\n        AddBatchDimensionProcessorStep(),\\n        relative_step,\\n        # NOTE: NormalizerProcessorStep MUST come before Pi05PrepareStateTokenizerProcessorStep\\n        # because the tokenizer step expects normalized state in [-1, 1] range for discretization\\n        NormalizerProcessorStep(\\n            features={**config.input_features, **config.output_features},\\n            norm_map=config.normalization_mapping,\\n            stats=dataset_stats,\\n        ),\\n        Pi05PrepareStateTokenizerProcessorStep(max_state_dim=config.max_state_dim),\\n        TokenizerProcessorStep(\\n            tokenizer_name=\\\"google/paligemma-3b-pt-224\\\",\\n            max_length=config.tokenizer_max_length,\\n            padding_side=\\\"right\\\",\\n            padding=\\\"max_length\\\",\\n        ),\\n        DeviceProcessorStep(device=config.device),\\n    ]\\n\\n    output_steps: list[ProcessorStep] = [\\n        UnnormalizerProcessorStep(\\n            features=config.output_features, norm_map=config.normalization_mapping, stats=dataset_stats\\n        ),\\n        AbsoluteActionsProcessorStep(enabled=config.use_relative_actions, relative_step=relative_step),\\n        DeviceProcessorStep(device=\\\"cpu\\\"),\\n    ]\\n\\n    return (\\n        PolicyProcessorPipeline[dict[str, Any], dict[str, Any]](\\n            steps=input_steps,\\n            name=POLICY_PREPROCESSOR_DEFAULT_NAME,\\n        ),\\n        PolicyProcessorPipeline[PolicyAction, PolicyAction](\\n            steps=output_steps,\\n            name=POLICY_POSTPROCESSOR_DEFAULT_NAME,\\n            to_transition=policy_action_to_transition,\\n            to_output=transition_to_policy_action,\\n        ),\\n    )\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.32}}"}}}

But MEAN_STD normalization doesn't guarantee values stay within [-1,1] bounds—it centers around zero with unit variance, so values can exceed that range.\n\nMy wrapper does the same normalization and discretization, and the prompt formatting matches exactly. The tokenizer implementation should also be equivalent.\n\nLet me look more carefully at the official `select_action` method to see what I might be missing.\n\nThe method calls `predict_action_chunk` which returns a batch of actions with shape `(1, 50, 7)`, then slices to `n_action_steps` (which is 50, so no change), transposes to `(50, 1, 7)`, and extends the queue with 50 individual action tensors of shape `(1, 7)`. The key insight is that these actions coming from the model are still in normalized form—they haven't been unnormalized yet. The unnormalization happens later when the evaluation harness applies the postprocessor to these queued actions.\n\nNow I'm wondering if there's a double unnormalization happening somewhere. The config shows ACTION uses MEAN_STD normalization during training, so the model learns to output normalized values. During inference, those normalized outputs need to be unnormalized back to the real action space. If the wrapper and official pipeline are using different unnormalization logic or applying it at different points, that could explain the performance gap. The model trains on normalized actions, and during inference the sample_actions method returns normalized predictions that the postprocessor then unnormalizes. Both the wrapper and the official postprocessor should be loading the same normalization statistics from the same files, so the unnormalization should match. Load LIBERO env + task suite\\\\n  2. Load SmolVLA (frozen) + CRM\\\\n  3. For each task, rollout N times:\\\\n     a. Reset env\\\\n     b. Each step: obs → SmolVLA K=10 samples → CGS on 6D pose → CRM\\\\n        → combine refined pose with gripper → execute 7D action\\\\n     c. Record success/failure\\\\n  4. Report per-task and overall success rate\\\\n\\\\nBaselines:\\\\n  - \\\\\\\"vla_only\\\\\\\": SmolVLA mean of K=10, no CRM refinement\\\\n  - \\\\\\\"crm_scalar\\\\\\\": CRM with scalar uncertainty (single std, not per-DOF)\\\\n  - \\\\\\\"crm_perdof\\\\\\\": CRM with full per-DOF uncertainty (ours)\\\\n  - \\\\\\\"acr_vla\\\\\\\": alias for crm_perdof — the full ACR-VLA pipeline\\\\n  - \\\\\\\"reconvla\\\\\\\": ReconVLA baseline — scalar Euclidean argmin selection (plan_014)\\\\n  - \\\\\\\"adaptive_k\\\\\\\": Adaptive K — K=2 L2 probe, expand to K=10 + CRM obs_only if uncertain\\\\n\\\\nAdaptive K mode (--mode adaptive_k):\\\\n  Each step: generate K=2 samples, compute pairwise L2 distance.\\\\n  If L2 > threshold (high uncertainty): generate 8 more (total K=10),\\\\n    select argmin(L2 to mean), apply CRM obs_only correction (epsilon=0.2).\\\\n  If L2 <= threshold (low uncertainty): use mean of K=2, skip CRM.\\\\n\\\\nUsage:\\\\n  python evaluate.py --benchmark object --checkpoint /path/to/crm_best.pt --n_rollouts 20\\\\n  python evaluate.py --benchmark object --mode vla_only --n_rollouts 20\\\\n  python evaluate.py --benchmark spatial --mode reconvla --n_rollouts 20\\\\n  python evaluate.py --benchmark spatial --mode acr_vla --crm_checkpoint /path/to/crm_best.pt --n_rollouts 10\\\\n\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\nimport argparse\\\\nimport functools\\\\nbuiltins_print = print\\\\nprint = functools.partial(builtins_print, flush=True)\\\\nimport os\\\\nos.environ.setdefault(\\\\\\\"HF_HUB_OFFLINE\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"1\\\\\\\")\\\\nos.environ.setdefault(\\\\\\\"TRANSFORMERS_OFFLINE\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"1\\\\\\\")\\\\nimport sys\\\\nimport json\\\\nimport time\\\\nimport signal\\\\nimport traceback\\\\nfrom pathlib import Path\\\\n\\\\nimport torch\\\\nimport numpy as np\\\\nfrom robosuite.utils.transform_utils import quat2axisangle\\\\n\\\\nsys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))\\\\nfrom cgs import ConformilizedGaussianScoring\\\\nfrom crm import ConformalRefinementModule\\\\nfrom aci import AdaptiveConformalInference\\\\nfrom utils import set_seed, CORRECTION_DIM, FULL_ACTION_DIM, H_EFF, OBS_DIM\\\\nfrom reconvla_baseline import reconvla_select\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef parse_args():\\\\n    p = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=\\\\\\\"Evaluate ACR-VLA on LIBERO\\\\\\\")\\\\n    p.add_argument(\\\\\\\"--benchmark\\\\\\\", type=str, default=\\\\\\\"object\\\\\\\",\\\\n                   choices=[\\\\\\\"object\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"spatial\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"goal\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"long\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"90\\\\\\\"])\\\\n    p.add_argument(\\\\\\\"--checkpoint\\\\\\\", type=str, default=None,\\\\n                   help=\\\\\\\"Path to CRM checkpoint (crm_best.pt)\\\\\\\")\\\\n    p.add_argument(\\\\\\\"--crm_checkpoint\\\\\\\", type=str, default=None,\\\\n                   help=\\\\\\\"Alias for --checkpoint (used with --mode acr_vla)\\\\\\\")\\\\n    p.add_argument(\\\\\\\"--n_rollouts\\\\\\\", type=int, default=20)\\\\n    p.add_argument(\\\\\\\"--max_steps\\\\\\\", type=int, default=300,\\\\n                   help=\\\\\\\"Max steps per rollout\\\\\\\")\\\\n    p.add_argument(\\\\\\\"--seed\\\\\\\", type=int, default=42)\\\\n    p.add_argument(\\\\\\\"--K\\\\\\\", type=int, default=10)\\\\n    p.add_argument(\\\\\\\"--mode\\\\\\\", type=str, default=\\\\\\\"crm_perdof\\\\\\\",\\\\n                   choices=[\\\\\\\"vla_only\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"crm_scalar\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"crm_perdof\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"acr_vla\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"crm_obs_only\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"reconvla\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"random_selection\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"adaptive_k\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"adaptive_unc_head\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"adaptive_conformal_crm\\\\\\\"])\\\\n    p.add_argument(\\\\\\\"--baseline_mode\\\\\\\", type=str, default=\\\\\\\"single\\\\\\\",\\\\n                   choices=[\\\\\\\"single\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"mean\\\\\\\"],\\\\n                   help=\\\\\\\"vla_only baseline: single (K=1) or mean (K=10 avg)\\\\\\\")\\\\n    p.add_argument(\\\\\\\"--smolvla_path\\\\\\\", type=str, default=\\\\\\\"/root/autodl-tmp/models/smolvla_libero/\\\\\\\")\\\\n    p.add_argument(\\\\\\\"--model\\\\\\\", type=str, default=\\\\\\\"smolvla\\\\\\\",\\\\n                   choices=[\\\\\\\"smolvla\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"pi05\\\\\\\"],\\\\n                   help=\\\\\\\"VLA model backbone\\\\\\\")\\\\n    p.add_argument(\\\\\\\"--pi05_path\\\\\\\", type=str,\\\\n                   default=\\\\\\\"/root/autodl-tmp/models/pi05_libero_finetuned/\\\\\\\",\\\\n                   help=\\\\\\\"Path to PI05 model (used when --model pi05)\\\\\\\")\\\\n    p.add_argument(\\\\\\\"--obs_dim\\\\\\\", type=int, default=OBS_DIM)\\\\n    p.add_argument(\\\\\\\"--hidden_dim\\\\\\\", type=int, default=256)\\\\n    p.add_argument(\\\\\\\"--task_filter\\\\\\\", t\\\\n\\\\n... [截断: 输出过长，省略 11885 字符] ...\\\\n\\\\new,\\\\n        task_name=task_name,\\\\n        robot_state=robot_state,\\\\n        eye_in_hand_rgb=eye_in_hand,\\\\n        K=1,\\\\n    )\\\\n    k_full = samples[0, :, :H_EFF, :]  # (1, H_eff, 7)\\\\n    vla_pose = k_full[0, :, :CORRECTION_DIM]  # (H_eff, 6)\\\\n\\\\n    obs_feat_dev = obs_feat.to(device)\\\\n    if obs_proj is not None:\\\\n        obs_feat_dev = obs_proj(obs_feat_dev)\\\\n    with torch.no_grad():\\\\n        u = unc_head(obs_feat_dev).item()\\\\n\\\\n    if u <= unc_threshold:\\\\n        mean_gripper = k_full[:, :, CORRECTION_DIM:].mean(dim=0)\\\\n        action_full = torch.cat([vla_pose, mean_gripper], dim=-1)\\\\n        step_metadata.append({\\\\\\\"uncertainty\\\\\\\": u, \\\\\\\"triggered\\\\\\\": False})\\\\n        return action_full[0].cpu().numpy()\\\\n    else:\\\\n        base_action = vla_pose.reshape(1, -1).to(device)  # (1, 30)\\\\n        with torch.no_grad():\\\\n            refined_pose = crm(base_action, None, obs_feat_dev)\\\\n        refined_pose = refined_pose[0].reshape(H_EFF, CORRECTION_DIM)\\\\n        mean_gripper = k_full[:, :, CORRECTION_DIM:].mean(dim=0)\\\\n        refined_full = torch.cat([refined_pose, mean_gripper], dim=-1)\\\\n        step_metadata.append({\\\\\\\"uncertainty\\\\\\\": u, \\\\\\\"triggered\\\\\\\": True})\\\\n        return refined_full[0].cpu().numpy()\\\\n\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef select_action_conformal_crm(wrapper, obs, task_name, crm, device,\\\\n                                 conformal_threshold, step_metadata, obs_proj=None):\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"K=2 Conformal + Selective CRM: always K=2, CRM only when uncertain.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    agentview = obs[\\\\\\\"agentview_image\\\\\\\"]\\\\n    eye_in_hand = obs.get(\\\\\\\"robot0_eye_in_hand_image\\\\\\\", None)\\\\n    robot_state = get_robot_state(obs)\\\\n\\\\n    obs_feat, samples = wrapper.get_obs_features_and_samples(\\\\n        agentview_rgb=agentview,\\\\n        task_name=task_name,\\\\n        robot_state=robot_state,\\\\n        eye_in_hand_rgb=eye_in_hand,\\\\n        K=2,\\\\n    )\\\\n    k_full = samples[0, :, :H_EFF, :]  # (2, H_eff, 7)\\\\n    k_pose = k_full[:, :, :CORRECTION_DIM]  # (2, H_eff, 6)\\\\n\\\\n    a1 = k_pose[0].reshape(-1)  # (30,)\\\\n    a2 = k_pose[1].reshape(-1)  # (30,)\\\\n    uncertainty = (a1 - a2).norm().item()\\\\n\\\\n    centroid = (a1 + a2) / 2\\\\n    d1 = (a1 - centroid).norm()\\\\n    d2 = (a2 - centroid).norm()\\\\n    selected_idx = 0 if d1 <= d2 else 1\\\\n    selected_pose = k_pose[selected_idx]  # (H_eff, 6)\\\\n\\\\n    triggered = uncertainty > conformal_threshold\\\\n\\\\n    if triggered:\\\\n        base_action = selected_pose.reshape(1, -1).to(device)  # (1, 30)\\\\n        obs_feat_dev = obs_feat.to(device)\\\\n        if obs_proj is not None:\\\\n            obs_feat_dev = obs_proj(obs_feat_dev)\\\\n        with torch.no_grad():\\\\n            refined_pose = crm(base_action, None, obs_feat_dev)\\\\n        refined_pose = refined_pose[0].reshape(H_EFF, CORRECTION_DIM)\\\\n        mean_gripper = k_full[:, :, CORRECTION_DIM:].mean(dim=0)\\\\n        action_full = torch.cat([refined_pose, mean_gripper], dim=-1)\\\\n    else:\\\\n        mean_gripper = k_full[:, :, CORRECTION_DIM:].mean(dim=0)\\\\n        action_full = torch.cat([selected_pose, mean_gripper], dim=-1)\\\\n\\\\n    step_metadata.append({\\\\n        \\\\\\\"uncertainty\\\\\\\": uncertainty,\\\\n        \\\\\\\"triggered\\\\\\\": triggered,\\\\n    })\\\\n    return action_full[0].cpu().numpy()\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef rollout_single(env, init_state, action_fn, max_steps=300, wrapper=None):\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Run one rollout. action_fn returns 7D action directly.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    env.reset()\\\\n    # Chunk-emitting wrappers (PI05) cache an action chunk across calls; flush\\\\n    # it here so a new rollout starts with a fresh inference.\\\\n    if wrapper is not None and hasattr(wrapper, \\\\\\\"reset_action_queue\\\\\\\"):\\\\n        wrapper.reset_action_queue()\\\\n    obs = env.set_init_state(init_state)\\\\n    for _ in range(5):\\\\n        obs, _, _, _ = env.step(np.zeros(7))\\\\n\\\\n    for step in range(max_steps):\\\\n        try:\\\\n            action_7d = action_fn(obs)\\\\n            obs, reward, done, info = env.step(action_7d)\\\\n        except Exception as e:\\\\n            print(f\\\\\\\"[rollout] Exception at step {step}: {e}\\\\\\\", flush=True)\\\\n            traceback.print_exc()\\\\n            return False, step\\\\n\\\\n        if done or info.get(\\\\\\\"success\\\\\\\", False):\\\\n            return True, step + 1\\\\n\\\\n    return False, max_steps\\\\n\\\\n\\\\n\\\", \\\"stderr\\\": \\\"\\\", \\\"return_code\\\": 0}, \\\"error\\\": null, \\\"metadata\\\": {\\\"elapsed_seconds\\\": 0.39}}\"}"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"12460971-c2b7-496e-bf65-b1cba93fe402","uuid":"04b1cfdd-e23b-47aa-ae6b-747830f909f3","timestamp":"2026-05-21T07:08:24.106Z","tool_use_result":{"content":"{\"result\":\"{\\\"success\\\": true, \\\"data\\\": {\\\"stdout\\\": \\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\nLIBERO evaluation script for ACR-VLA.\\\\n\\\\nRollout loop:\\\\n  1. K=2 samples, compute pairwise L2 distance.\\n  If L2 > threshold (high uncertainty): generate 8 more (total K=10),\\n    select argmin(L2 to mean), apply CRM obs_only correction (epsilon=0.2).\\n  If {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01SmGhp3WZHfkQZUtzCjVfF5","type":"tool_result","content":"{\"result\":\"{\\\"success\\\": true, \\\"data\\\": {\\\"stdout\\\": \\\"    def embed_prefix(\\\\n        self, images, img_masks, tokens, masks\\\\n    ) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]:\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Embed images with SigLIP and language tokens with embedding layer.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        embs = []\\\\n        pad_masks = []\\\\n        att_masks = []\\\\n\\\\n        # Process images\\\\n        for img, img_mask in zip(images, img_masks, strict=True):\\\\n\\\\n            def image_embed_func(img):\\\\n                return self.paligemma_with_expert.embed_image(img)\\\\n\\\\n            img_emb = self._apply_checkpoint(image_embed_func, img)\\\\n            bsize, num_img_embs = img_emb.shape[:2]\\\\n\\\\n            embs.append(img_emb)\\\\n            pad_masks.append(img_mask[:, None].expand(bsize, num_img_embs))\\\\n            att_masks += [0] * num_img_embs\\\\n\\\\n        # Process language tokens\\\\n        def lang_embed_func(tokens):\\\\n            lang_emb = self.paligemma_with_expert.embed_language_tokens(tokens)\\\\n            return lang_emb\\\\n\\\\n        lang_emb = self._apply_checkpoint(lang_embed_func, tokens)\\\\n        embs.append(lang_emb)\\\\n        pad_masks.append(masks)\\\\n\\\\n        num_lang_embs = lang_emb.shape[1]\\\\n        att_masks += [0] * num_lang_embs\\\\n\\\\n        embs = torch.cat(embs, dim=1)\\\\n        pad_masks = torch.cat(pad_masks, dim=1)\\\\n        att_masks = torch.tensor(att_masks, dtype=torch.bool, device=pad_masks.device)\\\\n\\\\n        bsize = pad_masks.shape[0]\\\\n        att_masks = att_masks[None, :].expand(bsize, len(att_masks))\\\\n\\\\n        return embs, pad_masks, att_masks\\\\n\\\\n    def embed_suffix(self, noisy_actions, timestep):\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Embed noisy_actions, timestep to prepare for Expert Gemma processing.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        embs = []\\\\n        pad_masks = []\\\\n        att_masks = []\\\\n\\\\n        # Embed timestep using sine-cosine positional encoding\\\\n        time_emb = create_sinusoidal_pos_embedding(\\\\n            timestep,\\\\n            self.action_in_proj.out_features,\\\\n            min_period=self.config.min_period,\\\\n            max_period=self.config.max_period,\\\\n            device=timestep.device,\\\\n        )\\\\n        time_emb = time_emb.type(dtype=timestep.dtype)\\\\n\\\\n        # Fuse timestep + action information using an MLP\\\\n        def action_proj_func(noisy_actions):\\\\n            return self.action_in_proj(noisy_actions)\\\\n\\\\n        action_emb = self._apply_checkpoint(action_proj_func, noisy_actions)\\\\n\\\\n        def time_mlp_func(time_emb):\\\\n            x = self.time_mlp_in(time_emb)\\\\n            x = F.silu(x)\\\\n            x = self.time_mlp_out(x)\\\\n            return F.silu(x)\\\\n\\\\n        time_emb = self._apply_checkpoint(time_mlp_func, time_emb)\\\\n        action_time_emb = action_emb\\\\n        adarms_cond = time_emb\\\\n\\\\n        embs.append(action_time_emb)\\\\n        bsize, action_time_dim = action_time_emb.shape[:2]\\\\n        action_time_mask = torch.ones(bsize, action_time_dim, dtype=torch.bool, device=timestep.device)\\\\n        pad_masks.append(action_time_mask)\\\\n\\\\n        # Set attention masks so that image, language and state inputs do not attend to action tokens\\\\n        att_masks += [1] + ([0] * (self.config.chunk_size - 1))\\\\n\\\\n        embs = torch.cat(embs, dim=1)\\\\n        pad_masks = torch.cat(pad_masks, dim=1)\\\\n        att_masks = torch.tensor(att_masks, dtype=embs.dtype, device=embs.device)\\\\n        att_masks = att_masks[None, :].expand(bsize, len(att_masks))\\\\n\\\\n        return embs, pad_masks, att_masks, adarms_cond\\\\n\\\\n    def forward(self, images, img_masks, tokens, masks, actions, noise, time) -> Tensor:\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Do a full training forward pass and compute the loss.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        time_expanded = time[:, None, None]\\\\n        x_t = time_expanded * noise + (1 - time_expanded) * actions\\\\n        u_t = noise - actions\\\\n\\\\n        prefix_embs, prefix_pad_masks, prefix_att_masks = self.embed_prefix(images, img_masks, tokens, masks)\\\\n        suffix_embs, suffix_pad_masks, suffix_att_masks, adarms_cond = self.embed_suffix(x_t, time)\\\\n\\\\n        if (\\\\n            self.paligemma_with_expert.paligemma.model.language_model.layers[0].self_attn.q_proj.weight.dtype\\\\n            == torch.bfloat16\\\\n        ):\\\\n            suffix_embs = suffi\\\\n\\\\n... [截断: 输出过长，省略 18895 字符] ...\\\\n\\\\n       images.append(img)\\\\n            img_masks.append(mask)\\\\n\\\\n        return images, img_masks\\\\n\\\\n    def prepare_action(self, batch):\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Pad action\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        actions = pad_vector(batch[ACTION], self.config.max_action_dim)\\\\n        return actions\\\\n\\\\n    @torch.no_grad()\\\\n    def select_action(self, batch: dict[str, Tensor]) -> Tensor:\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Select a single action given environment observations.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        assert not self._rtc_enabled(), (\\\\n            \\\\\\\"RTC is not supported for select_action, use it with predict_action_chunk\\\\\\\"\\\\n        )\\\\n\\\\n        self.eval()\\\\n\\\\n        # Action queue logic for n_action_steps > 1\\\\n        if len(self._action_queue) == 0:\\\\n            actions = self.predict_action_chunk(batch)[:, : self.config.n_action_steps]\\\\n            # Transpose to get shape (n_action_steps, batch_size, action_dim)\\\\n            self._action_queue.extend(actions.transpose(0, 1))\\\\n\\\\n        return self._action_queue.popleft()\\\\n\\\\n    @torch.no_grad()\\\\n    def predict_action_chunk(self, batch: dict[str, Tensor], **kwargs: Unpack[ActionSelectKwargs]) -> Tensor:\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Predict a chunk of actions given environment observations.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        self.eval()\\\\n\\\\n        # Prepare inputs\\\\n        images, img_masks = self._preprocess_images(batch)\\\\n        tokens, masks = batch[f\\\\\\\"{OBS_LANGUAGE_TOKENS}\\\\\\\"], batch[f\\\\\\\"{OBS_LANGUAGE_ATTENTION_MASK}\\\\\\\"]\\\\n\\\\n        # Sample actions using the model (pass through RTC kwargs, no separate state needed for PI05)\\\\n        actions = self.model.sample_actions(images, img_masks, tokens, masks, **kwargs)\\\\n\\\\n        # Unpad actions to actual action dimension\\\\n        original_action_dim = self.config.output_features[ACTION].shape[0]\\\\n        actions = actions[:, :, :original_action_dim]\\\\n\\\\n        return actions\\\\n\\\\n    def forward(self, batch: dict[str, Tensor], reduction: str = \\\\\\\"mean\\\\\\\") -> tuple[Tensor, dict]:\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Run the batch through the model and compute the loss for training.\\\\n\\\\n        Args:\\\\n            batch: Training batch containing observations and actions.\\\\n            reduction: How to reduce the loss. Options:\\\\n                - \\\\\\\"mean\\\\\\\": Return scalar mean loss (default, backward compatible)\\\\n                - \\\\\\\"none\\\\\\\": Return per-sample losses of shape (batch_size,) for RA-BC weighting\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        # Prepare inputs\\\\n        images, img_masks = self._preprocess_images(batch)\\\\n        tokens, masks = batch[f\\\\\\\"{OBS_LANGUAGE_TOKENS}\\\\\\\"], batch[f\\\\\\\"{OBS_LANGUAGE_ATTENTION_MASK}\\\\\\\"]\\\\n\\\\n        actions = self.prepare_action(batch)\\\\n\\\\n        noise = self.model.sample_noise(actions.shape, actions.device)\\\\n        time = self.model.sample_time(actions.shape[0], actions.device)\\\\n\\\\n        # \\\"\\\\n    def train(self, mode: bool = True):\\\\n        super().train(mode)\\\\n        if self.freeze_vision_encoder:\\\\n            self.paligemma.model.vision_tower.eval()\\\\n        if self.train_expert_only:\\\\n            self.paligemma.eval()\\\\n\\\\n    def embed_image(self, image: torch.Tensor):\\\\n        # Vision tower and multi_modal_projector are kept in float32 (params_to_keep_float32).\\\\n        out_dtype = image.dtype\\\\n        if image.dtype != torch.float32:\\\\n            image = image.to(torch.float32)\\\\n        image_outputs = self.paligemma.model.get_image_features(image)\\\\n        features = image_outputs.pooler_output\\\\n        if features.dtype != out_dtype:\\\\n            features = features.to(out_dtype)\\\\n        return features\\\\n\\\\n    def embed_language_tokens(self, tokens: torch.Tensor):\\\\n        return self.paligemma.model.language_model.get_input_embeddings()(tokens)\\\\n\\\\n    def forward(\\\\n        self,\\\\n        attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,\\\\n        position_ids: torch.LongTensor | None = None,\\\\n        past_key_values: list[torch.FloatTensor] | None = None,\\\\n        inputs_embeds: list[torch.FloatTensor] | None = None,\\\\n        use_cache: bool | None = None,\\\\n        adarms_cond: list[torch.Tensor] | None = None,\\\\n    ):\\\\n        if adarms_cond is None:\\\\n            adarms_cond = [None, None]\\\\n        if inputs_embeds[1] is None:\\\\n            prefix_output = self.paligemma.model.language_model.forward(\\\\n max_act_dim), wrapper.device)\nprint(f\"\\nnoise shape: {noise.shape}, mean: {noise.mean().item():.6f}, std: {noise.std().item():.6f}\")\n\nx_t = noise.clone()\ndt = -1.0 / num_steps\nfor step in prefix_pad_masks=pad_masks, past_key_values=kv_cache,\n        x_t=x_t, timestep=time_tensor,\n    )\n    x_t = x_t + dt * v_t\n\nraw_action = x_t[0, :, :7]  # (50, 7) - raw normalized\nunnorm_action = wrapper._unnormalize_action(x_t)[0]  # (50, 7) - unnormalized\n\nprint(f\"\\n=== Raw (normalized) actions ===\")\nprint(f\"shape: {raw_action.shape}\")\nprint(f\"step 0: {raw_action[0].cpu().numpy()}\")\nprint(f\"step 1: {raw_action[1].cpu().numpy()}\")\nprint(f\"mean per dim: {raw_action.mean(dim=0).cpu().numpy()}\")\nprint(f\"std per dim:  {raw_action.std(dim=0).cpu().numpy()}\")\nprint(f\"range: [{raw_action.min().item():.4f}, {raw_action.max().item():.4f}]\")\n\nprint(f\"\\n=== Unnormalized actions ===\")\nprint(f\"step 0: {unnorm_action[0].cpu().numpy()}\")\nprint(f\"step 1: {unnorm_action[1].cpu().numpy()}\")\nprint(f\"mean per dim: {unnorm_action.mean(dim=0).cpu().numpy()}\")\nprint(f\"std per dim:  {unnorm_action.std(dim=0).cpu().numpy()}\")\nprint(f\"range: [{unnorm_action.min().item():.4f}, {unnorm_action.max().item():.4f}]\")\n\n# Now try through official PI05Policy path (manual, avoiding torch.compile)\nprint(f\"\\n=== Official path comparison ===\")\n# Use same preprocessed inputs, same noise, but go through model.sample_actions logic manually\n# This is identical to what we just did above, so instead let's check the official postprocessor\nfrom safetensors.torch import load_file\npost_file = sorted(wrapper.config._pretrained_path.glob(\"*unnormalizer*processor*.safetensors\"))[0] if hasattr(wrapper.config, '_pretrained_path') else None\nif post_file is None:\n    from pathlib import Path\n    post_files = sorted(Path(\"/root/autodl-tmp/models/pi05_libero_finetuned/\").glob(\"*unnormalizer*processor*.safetensors\"))\n    if post_files:\n        post_file = post_files[0]\nif post_file:\n    post_stats = load_file(str(post_file))\n    print(f\"Post stats keys: {list(post_stats.keys())}\")\n    for k, v in post_stats.items():\n        print(f\"  {k}: shape={v.shape}, values={v.numpy()}\")\n\nenv.close()\nprint(f\"\\n[{time.time()-t0:.1f}s] Done.\")\nPYEOF\necho \"Script Prefetch next task's workers *after* closing current env to prevent\\n                # GPU memory overlap between consecutive tasks.\\n                if i + 1 < len(tasks):\\n                    next_env = tasks[i + 1][2]\\n                    if hasattr(next_env, \\\"_ensure\\\"):\\n                        prefetch_thread = threading.Thread(target=next_env._ensure, daemon=True)\\n                        prefetch_thread.start()\\n    else:\\n        with cf.ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=max_parallel_tasks) as executor:\\n            fut2meta = {}\\n            for task_group, task_id, env in tasks:\\n                fut = executor.submit(task_runner, task_group, task_id, env)\\n                fut2meta[fut] = (task_group, task_id, env)\\n            for fut in cf.as_completed(fut2meta):\\n                tg, tid, env = fut2meta[fut]\\n                try:\\n                    tg, tid, metrics = fut.result()\\n                    _accumulate_to(tg, metrics)\\n                    per_task_infos.append({\\\"task_group\\\": tg, \\\"task_id\\\": tid, \\\"metrics\\\": metrics})\\n                finally:\\n                    env.close()\\n\\n    # compute aggregated metrics helper (robust to lists/scalars)\\n    def _agg_from_list(xs):\\n        if not xs:\\n            return float(\\\"nan\\\")\\n        arr = np.array(xs, dtype=float)\\n        return float(np.nanmean(arr))\\n\\n    # compute per-group aggregates\\n    groups_aggregated = {}\\n    for group, acc in group_acc.items():\\n        groups_aggregated[group] = {\\n            \\\"avg_sum_reward\\\": _agg_from_list(acc[\\\"sum_rewards\\\"]),\\n            \\\"avg_max_reward\\\": _agg_from_list(acc[\\\"max_rewards\\\"]),\\n            \\\"pc_success\\\": _agg_from_list(acc[\\\"successes\\\"]) * 100 if acc[\\\"successes\\\"] else float(\\\"nan\\\"),\\n            \\\"n_episodes\\\": len(acc[\\\"sum_rewards\\\"]),\\n            \\\"video_paths\\\": list(acc[\\\"video_paths\\\"]),\\n        }\\n\\n    # overall aggregates\\n    overall_agg = {\\n        \\\"avg_sum_reward\\\": _agg_from_list(overall[\\\"sum_rewards\\\"]),\\n        \\\"avg_max_reward\\\": _agg_from_list(overall[\\\"max_rewards\\\"]),\\n        \\\"pc_success\\\": _agg_from_list(overall[\\\"successes\\\"]) * 100 if overall[\\\"successes\\\"] else float(\\\"nan\\\"),\\n        \\\"n_episodes\\\": len(overall[\\\"sum_rewards\\\"]),\\n        \\\"eval_s\\\": time.time() - start_t,\\n        \\\"eval_ep_s\\\": (time.time() - start_t) / max(1, len(overall[\\\"sum_rewards\\\"])),\\n        \\\"video_paths\\\": list(overall[\\\"video_paths\\\"]),\\n    }\\n\\n    return {\\n        \\\"per_task\\\": per_task_infos,\\n        \\\"per_group\\\": groups_aggregated,\\n        \\\"overall\\\": overall_agg,\\n    }\\n\\n\\ndef main():\\n    init_logging()\\n    register_third_party_plugins()\\n    eval_main()\\n\\n\\nif __name__ == \\\"__main__\\\":\\n    main()\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.37}}"}}}

In the latter case,\\\\n            the repo must include a Python file (usually `env.py`).\\\\n        n_envs (int, optional): The number of parallelized env to return. Defaults to 1.\\\\n        use_async_envs (bool, optional): Whether to return an AsyncVectorEnv or a SyncVectorEnv. Defaults to\\\\n            False.\\\\n        hub_cache_dir (str | None): Optional cache path for downloaded hub files.\\\\n        trust_remote_code (bool): **Explicit consent** to execute remote code from the Hub.\\\\n            Default False — must be set to True to import/exec hub `env.py`.\\\\n    Raises:\\\\n        ValueError: if n_envs < 1\\\\n        ModuleNotFoundError: If the requested env package is not installed\\\\n\\\\n    Returns:\\\\n        dict[str, dict[int, gym.vector.VectorEnv]]:\\\\n            A mapping from suite name to indexed vectorized environments.\\\\n            - For multi-task benchmarks (e.g., LIBERO): one entry per suite, and one vec env per task_id.\\\\n            - For single-task environments: a single suite entry (cfg.type) with task_id=0.\\\\n\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    # import and surface clear import errors\\\\n        module = _import_hub_module(local_file, repo_id)\\\\n\\\\n        # call the hub-provided make_env\\\\n        env_cfg = None if isinstance(cfg, str) else cfg\\\\n        raw_result = _call_make_env(module, n_envs=n_envs, use_async_envs=use_async_envs, cfg=env_cfg)\\\\n\\\\n        # normalize the return into {suite: {task_id: vec_env}}\\\\n        return _normalize_hub_result(raw_result)\\\\n\\\\n    # At this point, cfg must be an EnvConfig (not a string) since hub_path would have been set otherwise\\\\n    if isinstance(cfg, str):\\\\n        raise TypeError(\\\\\\\"cfg should be an EnvConfig at this point\\\\\\\")\\\\n\\\\n    if n_envs < 1:\\\\n        raise ValueError(\\\\\\\"`n_envs` must be at least 1\\\\\\\")\\\\n\\\\n    return cfg.create_envs(n_envs=n_envs, use_async_envs=use_async_envs)\\\\n\\\", \\\"stderr\\\": \\\"\\\", \\\"return_code\\\": 0}, \\\"error\\\": null, \\\"metadata\\\": {\\\"elapsed_seconds\\\": 0.32}}\"}","structuredContent":{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"#!/usr/bin/env python\\n\\n# Copyright 2024 The HuggingFace Inc. team. All rights reserved.\\n#\\n# Licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the \\\"License\\\");\\n# you may not use this file except in compliance with the License.\\n# You may obtain a copy of the License at\\n#\\n#     http://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0\\n#\\n# Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing, software\\n# distributed under the License is distributed on an \\\"AS IS\\\" BASIS,\\n# WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY KIND, either express or implied.\\n# See the License for the specific language governing permissions and\\n# limitations under the License.\\nfrom __future__ import annotations\\n\\nfrom typing import Any\\n\\nimport gymnasium as gym\\n\\nfrom .configs import EnvConfig, HubEnvConfig\\nfrom .utils import _call_make_env, _download_hub_file, _import_hub_module, _normalize_hub_result\\n\\n\\ndef make_env_config(env_type: str, **kwargs) -> EnvConfig:\\n    try:\\n        cls = EnvConfig.get_choice_class(env_type)\\n    except KeyError as err:\\n        raise ValueError(\\n            f\\\"Environment type '{env_type}' is not registered. \\\"\\n            f\\\"Available: {list(EnvConfig.get_known_choices().keys())}\\\"\\n        ) from err\\n    return cls(**kwargs)\\n\\n\\ndef make_env_pre_post_processors(\\n    env_cfg: EnvConfig,\\n    policy_cfg: Any,\\n) -> tuple[Any, Any]:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    Create preprocessor and postprocessor pipelines for environment observations.\\n\\n    Returns a tuple of (preprocessor, postprocessor). By default, delegates to\\n    ``env_cfg.get_env_processors()``.  The XVLAConfig policy-specific override\\n    stays here because it depends on the *policy* config, not the env config.\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    from lerobot.policies.xvla.configuration_xvla import XVLAConfig\\n\\n    if isinstance(policy_cfg, XVLAConfig):\\n        from lerobot.policies.xvla.processor_xvla import make_xvla_libero_pre_post_processors\\n\\n        return make_xvla_libero_pre_post_processors()\\n\\n    return env_cfg.get_env_processors()\\n\\n\\ndef make_env(\\n    cfg: EnvConfig | str,\\n    n_envs: int = 1,\\n    use_async_envs: bool = False,\\n    hub_cache_dir: str | None = None,\\n    trust_remote_code: bool = False,\\n) -> dict[str, dict[int, gym.vector.VectorEnv]]:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Makes a gym vector environment according to the config or Hub reference.\\n\\n    Args:\\n        cfg (EnvConfig | str): Either an `EnvConfig` object describing the environment to build locally,\\n            or a Hugging Face Hub repository identifier (e.g. `\\\"username/repo\\\"`). In the latter case,\\n            the repo must include a Python file (usually `env.py`).\\n        n_envs (int, optional): The number of parallelized env to return. Defaults to 1.\\n        use_async_envs (bool, optional): Whether to return an AsyncVectorEnv or a SyncVectorEnv. Defaults to\\n            False.\\n        hub_cache_dir (str | None): Optional cache path for downloaded hub files.\\n        trust_remote_code (bool): **Explicit consent** to execute remote code from the Hub.\\n            Default False — must be set to True to import/exec hub `env.py`.\\n    Raises:\\n        ValueError: if n_envs < 1\\n        ModuleNotFoundError: If the requested env package is not installed\\n\\n    Returns:\\n        dict[str, dict[int, gym.vector.VectorEnv]]:\\n            A mapping from suite name to indexed vectorized environments.\\n            - For multi-task benchmarks (e.g., LIBERO): one entry per suite, and one vec env per task_id.\\n            - For single-task environments: a single suite entry (cfg.type) with task_id=0.\\n\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    # if user passed a hub id string (e.g., \\\"username/repo\\\", \\\"username/repo@main:env.py\\\")\\n    # simplified: only support hub-provided `make_env`\\n    # TODO: (jadechoghari): deprecate string API and remove this check\\n    if isinstance(cfg, str):\\n        hub_path: str | None = cfg\\n    elif isinstance(cfg, HubEnvConfig):\\n        hub_path = cfg.hub_path\\n    else:\\n        hub_path = None\\n\\n    # If hub_path is set, download and call hub-provided `make_env`\\n    if hub_path:\\n        # _download_hub_file will raise the same RuntimeError if trust_remote_code is False\\n        repo_id, file_path, local_file, revision = _download_hub_file(\\n            hub_path, trust_remote_code, hub_cache_dir\\n        )\\n\\n        # import and surface clear import errors\\n        module = _import_hub_module(local_file, repo_id)\\n\\n        # call the hub-provided make_env\\n        env_cfg = None if isinstance(cfg, str) else cfg\\n        raw_result = _call_make_env(module, n_envs=n_envs, use_async_envs=use_async_envs, cfg=env_cfg)\\n\\n        # normalize the return into {suite: {task_id: vec_env}}\\n        return _normalize_hub_result(raw_result)\\n\\n    # At this point, cfg must be an EnvConfig (not a string) since hub_path would have been set otherwise\\n    if isinstance(cfg, str):\\n        raise TypeError(\\\"cfg should be an EnvConfig at this point\\\")\\n\\n    if n_envs < 1:\\n        raise ValueError(\\\"`n_envs` must be at least 1\\\")\\n\\n    return cfg.create_envs(n_envs=n_envs, use_async_envs=use_async_envs)\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.32}}"}}}

permissions and\\\\n# limitations under the License.\\\\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass\\\\n\\\\nimport torch\\\\n\\\\nfrom lerobot.configs import FeatureType, PipelineFeatureType, PolicyFeature\\\\nfrom lerobot.utils.constants import OBS_IMAGES, OBS_PREFIX, OBS_STATE, OBS_STR\\\\n\\\\nfrom .pipeline import ObservationProcessorStep, ProcessorStepRegistry\\\\n\\\\n\\\\n@dataclass\\\\n@ProcessorStepRegistry.register(name=\\\\\\\"libero_processor\\\\\\\")\\\\nclass LiberoProcessorStep(ObservationProcessorStep):\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    Processes LIBERO observations into the LeRobot format.\\\\n\\\\n    This step handles the specific observation structure from LIBERO environments,\\\\n    which includes nested robot_state dictionaries and image observations.\\\\n\\\\n    **State Processing:**\\\\n    -   Processes the `robot_state` dictionary which contains nested end-effector,\\\\n        gripper, and joint information.\\\\n    -   Extracts and concatenates:\\\\n        - End-effector position (3D)\\\\n        - End-effector quaternion converted to axis-angle (3D)\\\\n        - Gripper joint positions (2D)\\\\n    -   Maps the concatenated state to `\\\\\\\"observation.state\\\\\\\"`.\\\\n\\\\n    **Image Processing:**\\\\n    -   Rotates images by 180 degrees by flipping both height and width dimensions.\\\\n    -   This accounts for the HuggingFaceVLA/libero camera orientation convention.\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n    def _process_observation(self, observation):\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        Processes both image and robot_state observations from LIBERO.\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        processed_obs = observation.copy()\\\\n        for key in list(processed_obs.keys()):\\\\n            if key.startswith(f\\\\\\\"{OBS_IMAGES}.\\\\\\\"):\\\\n                img = processed_obs[key]\\\\n\\\\n                # Flip both H and W\\\\n                img = torch.flip(img, dims=[2, 3])\\\\n\\\\n                processed_obs[key] = img\\\\n        # Process robot_state into a flat state vector\\\\n        observation_robot_state_str = OBS_PREFIX + \\\\\\\"robot_state\\\\\\\"\\\\n        if observation_robot_state_str in processed_obs:\\\\n            robot_state = processed_obs.pop(observation_robot_state_str)\\\\n\\\\n            # Extract components\\\\n            eef_pos = robot_state[\\\\\\\"eef\\\\\\\"][\\\\\\\"pos\\\\\\\"]  # (B, 3,)\\\\n            eef_quat = robot_state[\\\\\\\"eef\\\\\\\"][\\\\\\\"quat\\\\\\\"]  # (B, 4,)\\\\n            gripper_qpos = robot_state[\\\\\\\"gripper\\\\\\\"][\\\\\\\"qpos\\\\\\\"]  # (B, 2,)\\\\n\\\\n            # Convert quaternion to axis-angle\\\\n            eef_axisangle = self._quat2axisangle(eef_quat)  # (B, 3)\\\\n            # Concatenate into a single state vector\\\\n            state = torch.cat((eef_pos, eef_axisangle, gripper_qpos), dim=-1)\\\\n\\\\n            # ensure float32\\\\n            state = state.float()\\\\n            if state.dim() == 1:\\\\n                state = state.unsqueeze(0)\\\\n\\\\n            processed_obs[OBS_STATE] = state\\\\n        return processed_obs\\\\n\\\\n    def transform_features(\\\\n        self, features: dict[PipelineFeatureType, dict[str, PolicyFeature]]\\\\n    ) -> dict[PipelineFeatureType, dict[str, PolicyFeature]]:\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        Transforms feature keys from the LIBERO format to the LeRobot standard.\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        new_features: dict[PipelineFeatureType, dict[str, PolicyFeature]] = {}\\\\n\\\\n        # copy over non-STATE features\\\\n        for ft, feats in features.items():\\\\n            if ft != FeatureType.STATE:\\\\n                new_features[ft] = feats.copy()\\\\n\\\\n        # rebuild STATE features\\\\n        state_feats = {}\\\\n\\\\n        # add our new flattened state\\\\n        state_feats[OBS_STATE] = PolicyFeature(\\\\n            type=FeatureType.STATE,\\\\n            shape=(8,),  # [eef_pos(3), axis_angle(3), gripp\\\\n\\\\n...
